# Fix ledger — every edit the manual audit says is needed (2026-10-29)

**Developer instruction, 2026-10-29:** *"Make a record-file for notating all the necessary edits that are required as you go through each city, and then once all the checks are done, then run through and apply the fixes all in one sweep."*

**So:** during the audit **nothing is edited**. Every needed change is written here, city by city, the moment it is found. **After the last city is checked, one sweep applies them all** (order below). The evidence for each entry is in that city's `<City>_Audit.md` (the "Ref" column gives the finding ID there).

## ⭐ SWEEP STATUS (2026-10-30)
**Developer, 2026-10-30:** *"first, go through and make all the edits for things that need fixing. Then, we'll go through one city at a time and take a look at the things that require my attention."* **Done:** every `PENDING` entry was applied (or marked `PARTIAL` / `HELD` / `SKIPPED-MISMATCH` with the reason) by one helper per city, using `SWEEP_BRIEF.md`; each city's report is in `Sweep_Reports/<City>_Sweep.md`, and `Sweep_Reports/PROGRESS.md` collects what each helper found beyond the ledger. **Left for the walk-through: all `DECIDE` entries** (they are untouched) plus the new items in `PROGRESS.md`. Status words below: `APPLIED` · `PARTIAL` (what is left is stated in the report) · `ALREADY-RIGHT` (the old text was not in that city's files) · `SKIPPED-MISMATCH` (the live source differs from this ledger — the ledger was wrong; see the report) · `DECIDE` (waits for the developer). **Where a status cell says `APPLIED`, the helper re-read the live source line before writing; the ledger text in the "Where / current" and "Change" cells is the audit's reading of 2026-10-29/30 and is NOT corrected here where a helper found it wrong — the report says what the live file holds.** Nothing is committed.

## How to read an entry
`ID` · `Kind` · `Target file` · `Where` (the section, row or line, with a snippet of the CURRENT text so the spot can be found even if lines move) · `Change` · `Ref` · `Status`.

**Kinds.** `VALUE` a figure or fact in a sheet that does not match its source · `REPOINT` a line number that no longer lands on the cited text (the **anchor text** of the correct line is recorded, so the number is re-read from the live file at sweep time, never copied from here) · `ADD` a missing row or address · `REWORD` wording that misstates the source or breaks a standing rule · `BUILDER` a fix to the generator's text, which every city's sheet inherits · `SOURCE` a correction to a canon or spec file (**needs the developer's decision; never applied on the sweep's own authority**) · `AMEND` a finished city's pass (or any finished record) rests on an input that has since changed: **the pass file is never edited**; the entry says what changed and where an amendment note is to be filed beside the pass (the way the Mirny and Davis amendment registers work).

**Status.** `PENDING` (found, not yet applied) · `DECIDE` (waits for a developer answer) · `APPROVED` · `APPLIED` (date) · `DECLINED`.

## The sweep (run once, after the last city is checked)
1. **Resolve every `DECIDE` entry** with the developer (a short list, grouped).
2. **`SOURCE` edits that are approved**, in place, keeping line counts unchanged wherever possible (a replaced word or number, not an added paragraph), so citations are not disturbed again.
3. **`BUILDER` edits** to `Tools/build_pretrip.py` and `Tools/build_datasheets.py` (so a future rebuild is right). ⛔ **Do NOT rebuild any city with `--force`:** it drops the hand-added rows (Santa Luce, Relung Panen). Apply the changes to each sheet in place.
4. **`VALUE`, `REWORD` and `ADD` edits** to every sheet, city by city, copying new text from the live source line (not from this file).
5. **`REPOINT` last**, for every sheet: re-open each cited line, find its anchor text, write the live number. Then re-read every changed row against its source (a hand check, same standard as the audit).
6. Update `Datasheet_and_PreTrip_Tracker.md`, mark each entry `APPLIED` with the date, re-audit one city end to end as a spot check. **No commit unless the developer asks.**

**Standing rules while sweeping:** American English; no post-war content in a datasheet; no cross-city comparison; a datasheet row is value + citation only; finished-city pass files are not edited; the timeline repo takes no worldbuilding edits (flag only).
